### Smart Home project proforma workflow

Added the desktop Smart Home project offer flow inside the Sales Hub, with customer and project fields, block and flat counts, a searchable multi-select product pool, per-product quantities and prices, currency-aware totals, and PDF generation through the existing authenticated proforma endpoint. Mixed-currency PDF items convert with configured exchange rates; missing conversion rates stop generation. Shared workflow tabs connect Security Systems, Smart Home, and PC Builder. Hidden Edge verified two selected products across EUR and USD, the converted `bulut_deri` PDF request, popup delivery, the 390px responsive layout, and zero browser exceptions. Sales-focused regressions pass (17 tests); syntax, ASCII, mojibake, and diff checks pass.

- Central backup trigger parity (2026-10-17): Admin Konsol can now create a tenant-scoped SQLite snapshot from the selected live tenant using SQLite's online backup API. It validates integrity, stores through the existing `support_backups` registry with product, tenant, source, checksum, size, and platform identity metadata, and records an audit event. Both the live-company action and backup center button use the control-admin-only API. Focused isolation tests verify the snapshot contents, metadata, audit, role gate, and missing-tenant handling; authenticated HTTP coverage verifies anonymous denial and operator creation. The headless Edge audit triggers from both UI actions and verifies the tenant IDs sent to the API, alongside restore confirmation, tenant switching, download, mobile sizing, and zero browser exceptions.

- Company mute parity (2026-10-17): Customer 360 now confirms whole-tenant access suspension and restoration through the control-admin company API. It marks muted companies in the list and detail, preserves the selected company after updates, and prevents an operator from muting its own platform tenant in both UI and API. Authenticated HTTP checks cover self-mute denial, customer login denial while muted, restored login after unmute, and mute/unmute writes; hidden Edge verified confirmation, state labels, tenant selection retention, mobile width, and zero page exceptions.

- Customer 360 company archive parity (2026-10-17): enabled the existing control-admin archive route from the selected company detail. The action requires the exact company name, removes the tenant from the active list, and reports the archived database filename. The UI blocks the operator's own platform tenant; the API also rejects the platform tenant and any control-owner company. Focused unit and authenticated HTTP tests verify archive movement and registry removal; hidden Edge verified mismatch cancellation, no write on mismatch, successful archive request, selection fallback, and zero page exceptions.

- Customer 360 temporary password parity (2026-10-17): active tenant users with valid email addresses can receive an emailed one-time password from their user row. Confirmation explains session invalidation and the required first-login password change; failed delivery leaves the dialog open and reports the API error. The existing central API keeps the tenant-scoped credential update, expiry, audit, and send-before-commit behavior. Focused backend and authenticated HTTP checks pass; hidden Edge verified recipient tenant/user payload, confirmation, success toast, mobile layout, and zero page exceptions.

- Customer 360 reset-link targeting follow-up (2026-10-17): moved reset-link sending onto each user's row, so the selected user receives the link instead of silently targeting the first row. Confirmation names the recipient, delivery failure keeps the confirmation open, and the bearer URL is no longer shown in the admin page. Hidden Edge verified a second-row tenant/user payload, SMTP failure recovery, successful retry, hidden bearer URL, and zero page exceptions; focused UI and HTTP checks pass.
- Customer 360 missing-user recovery parity (2026-10-17): when a tenant has no users, the company detail exposes the existing protected-backup recovery endpoint with explicit confirmation. After recovery, it reports the restored user count and hides the action once users return. Focused recovery, authenticated HTTP, and Customer 360 tests pass; hidden Edge verified one restored user and zero page exceptions.
- Admin quota page workflow follow-up (2026-10-17): fixed the tenant search counter, which was updated by the filter handler but missing from the rendered page. Tenant-row quota actions now select that tenant in the existing upgrade panel, and the header action takes the user to its selector while preserving the selected tenant through the resulting control-center refresh. The upgrade mutation remains unavailable because the central license API does not support it; placeholder prices are suppressed so the web page does not present values outside the central license catalog. Focused quota tests pass, and hidden Edge verified filtering, row selection, refresh-safe selection, unavailable state, CSV export, mobile width, and zero page exceptions.
- Proforma legacy image migration (2026-10-17): saved templates with desktop file paths now show accessible replacement placeholders at the original image locations. Selecting a PNG/JPEG uses the existing bounded image import to embed a compressed copy; unresolved images block save/activation, and the browser never reads the old local path. The focused PC Builder reference suite passes (10 tests); hidden Edge verified the placeholder, no write while unresolved, in-place replacement, embedded template persistence, activation, and zero page exceptions.
- Sales Hub tabs (2026-10-17): aligned the shared web workflow tabs with the native Sales Hub tab labels and 34 px tab sizing. The PC Builder offscreen audit exposed an outdated geometry baseline from before the web tabs existed; its expected positions now include the 34 px native tab bar. Hidden Edge verified Sales, Smart Home, and PC Builder navigation and selected states, plus the existing PC Builder workflow and 390 px layout checks, with zero exceptions. Paired capture: `output/playwright/pc-builder-corrected-native-paired-headless.png`.
- Legacy license keygen dispatch (2026-10-17): replaced the silent redirect with a dialog explaining that the old local HWID generator is unavailable on web and directing the operator to central license settings. The focused dialog-dispatch suite passes (62 tests); hidden Edge verified the message, action, route, and offer-reversal interaction with zero page exceptions.
- Proforma legacy PDF path handoff (2026-10-17): the authenticated bootstrap exposes a boolean for a saved desktop PDF path without returning that path, and the template editor prompts the user to re-select the file. Uploading a PDF clears the notice; removing it surfaces the notice again if a legacy path remains. Focused API tests pass (12 total with PC Builder reference coverage); hidden Edge verified the notice, upload handoff, removal behavior, save/activation checks, 390 px notice fit, and zero exceptions.

- Customer 360 manual password reset parity (2026-10-17): added a per-user masked password dialog with the desktop six-character minimum. It calls the existing central reset endpoint with the selected tenant and user, which hashes the new password in that tenant database. Focused backend hash and unknown-tenant isolation tests pass. Hidden Edge verified short-password rejection with no request, the valid tenant-b/user-2 payload, success feedback, reset-link workflows, the 390 px layout, and zero page exceptions. The user-creation limitation remains because the desktop action navigates to Companies and the central API has no create-user operation.

## Service Status Classification and Metrics (2026-10-17)

- The status-board filters now match `ServiceStatusPage._status_is_delivered`: a status is delivered when it contains `TESLIM` and does not contain `HAZIR`; part-waiting states remain in the parts category, and canceled rows follow the desktop active-category filter behavior. Occupancy and critical metrics separately exclude canceled and returned rows.
- Sidebar service badges use their own desktop-equivalent alias rule from `src/utils/status_utils.py`: legacy `Tamamland\u0131` and `Haz\u0131r` records are excluded, while `Teslime Hazir` stays active.
- Replaced web status-card estimates with desktop calculations for today's accepted/delivered volume, delivered/active-today details, workshop occupancy and configured capacity, urgent/parts critical counts, and average delivery duration. Deleted and archived records are ignored; dates accept both ISO and `dd.MM.yyyy` formats.
